## Frequently asked questions

<details>
<summary><b>When do internships open?</b></summary>

All year round. Many companies post an intake months ahead, and others open a few weeks before the start. New internships arrive as we read company career pages every night, so check often and apply early.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Which companies run internship programs?</b></summary>

Big tech companies, banks, carmakers, labs, hospitals, shops and many small companies. The tables above list some of the companies with internships on Likeremote right now.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Is it too early to apply ahead of time?</b></summary>

Not if the role is open. Most companies post one intake ahead, so apply as soon as you see a role that fits you.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Can I do an internship if I graduate soon?</b></summary>

Some companies allow it, and others want you to go back to school afterwards. The advert says who can apply. If you graduate soon, look at entry-level roles too.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Are internships paid?</b></summary>

Some are and some are not. When the employer lists the pay, you see it in the Salary column.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>How many internships should I apply to?</b></summary>

There is no magic number. Apply to every role that fits you well instead of to everything. Your fit score on likeremote.com helps you choose.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>What if I don’t know which roles to apply for?</b></summary>

Start with a category above, or add your CV on likeremote.com and look at your best matches. They often show roles you had not thought of.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>How do I show impact with little experience?</b></summary>

Use school projects, clubs, part-time jobs and side projects. Say what you did and what changed, with a number where you can, like “cut page load time by half” or “ran a club of 40 people”.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Can international students do an internship in the US?</b></summary>

Often yes, but it depends on your visa and the employer. Many students on an F-1 visa use CPT for internships. Ask your school’s international office, and read what the advert asks for.

</details>

## Legend

- 🆕 posted in the last 24 hours
- **Salary** is shown when the employer lists it, otherwise **—**
- **Work mode**: On-site, Hybrid or Remote
- **Apply** opens the job page on likeremote.com
- 🔒 closed in the last 60 days; older closed rows are in [README-Closed.md](README-Closed.md)

## Add a role, report a closed one, fix a row

- [New internship](https://github.com/Likeremote-com-1-4M-job-board/Internships-Europe/issues/new?template=new-internship.yml)
- [Closed internship](https://github.com/Likeremote-com-1-4M-job-board/Internships-Europe/issues/new?template=closed-internship.yml)
- [Wrong data](https://github.com/Likeremote-com-1-4M-job-board/Internships-Europe/issues/new?template=wrong-data.yml)

A maintainer who approves an issue only records the link for us. It does not edit a row: this list is rebuilt from the likeremote.com board every 6 hours, so a role shows up here once the board has it.
